[ARTIFACT_CONTENT_BEGIN] llm_response_31f72478-575e-4db9-9fb1-2f1cce9b1e0f_0_Qwen_Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct-FP8.txt
```python
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class TestDotDSL(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_node(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_node_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_edge_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})

    def test_graph_with_one_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})

    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"}), Node("b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])

    def test_malformed_graph_data_not_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_malformed_graph_data_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph(None)
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_graph_item_incomplete_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_graph_item_incomplete_node_tuple_missing_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "a")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_graph_item_incomplete_edge_tuple_missing_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_graph_item_incomplete_attr_tuple_missing_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "foo")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_attribute_malformed_not_string_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_attribute_malformed_not_string_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 456)])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_node_malformed_not_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_node_malformed_not_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "a", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_edge_malformed_not_string_src(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_edge_malformed_not_string_dst(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", 456, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_edge_malformed_not_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", "not a dict")])
```

```python
import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDSLTest(unittest.TestCase):

    # 1) successful construction/usage
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        g = Graph([])
        self.assertEqual(g.data, [])

    def test_graph_with_single_node(self):
        g = Graph([(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.data), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.data[0], (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}))

    def test_graph_with_single_edge(self):
        g = Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.data), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.data[0], (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"}))

    def test_graph_with_single_attr(self):
        g = Graph([(ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow")])
        self.assertEqual(len(g.data), 1)
        self.assertEqual(g.data[0], (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"))

    def test_graph_with_mixed_items(self):
        data = [
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})
        ]
        g = Graph(data)
        self.assertEqual(g.data, data)

    # 2) invalid input type
    def test_graph_data_not_list_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph data malformed"):
            Graph("not a list")

    def test_graph_data_none_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph data malformed"):
            Graph(None)

    # 3) shape/arity errors (missing/extra elements)
    def test_graph_item_empty_tuple_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([()])

    def test_graph_item_single_element_tuple_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(NODE,)])

    def test_node_tuple_missing_attrs_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(NODE, "a")])

    def test_edge_tuple_missing_attrs_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b")])

    def test_attr_tuple_missing_value_raises_type_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(TypeError, "Graph item incomplete"):
            Graph([(ATTR, "bgcolor")])

    # 4) value/type validation errors (wrong types for fields)
    def test_node_with_non_string_name_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])

    def test_node_with_non_dict_attrs_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Node is malformed"):
            Graph([(NODE, "a", "not a dict")])

    def test_edge_with_non_string_src_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "b", {})])

    def test_edge_with_non_string_dst_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", 123, {})])

    def test_edge_with_non_dict_attrs_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Edge is malformed"):
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", "not a dict")])

    def test_attr_with_non_string_key_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Attribute is malformed"):
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])

    # 5) unknown/unsupported case
    def test_unknown_item_type_raises_value_error(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Unknown item"):
            Graph([(999, "a", "b", {})])
```

```python
import unittest
from main import Graph, Node, Edge, NODE, EDGE, ATTR


class DotDSLTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [])

    def test_graph_with_attributes(self):
        graph = Graph([(ATTR, "key", "value")])
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [(ATTR, "key", "value")])

    def test_graph_with_node(self):
        graph = Graph([(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [(NODE, "a", {"color": "red"})])

    def test_graph_with_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.data, [(EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])

    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "bgcolor", "yellow"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "red"}),
            (NODE, "b", {"color": "blue"}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(len(graph.data), 4)

    def test_invalid_graph_data_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")

    def test_graph_item_incomplete(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR,)])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")

    def test_malformed_attribute(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, 456)])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_malformed_node(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_malformed_edge(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(99, "unknown", "item")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")

    def test_node_equality(self):
        node1 = Node("a", {"color": "red"})
        node2 = Node("a", {"color": "red"})
        self.assertEqual(node1, node2)

    def test_edge_equality(self):
        edge1 = Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        edge2 = Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        self.assertEqual(edge1, edge2)

    def test_node_inequality(self):
        node1 = Node("a", {"color": "red"})
        node2 = Node("b", {"color": "red"})
        self.assertNotEqual(node1, node2)

    def test_edge_inequality(self):
        edge1 = Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        edge2 = Edge("a", "c", {"color": "blue"})
        self.assertNotEqual(edge1, edge2)

    def test_attribute_with_empty_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "", "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")

    def test_node_with_empty_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "", {"color": "red"})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")

    def test_edge_with_empty_nodes(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "", "", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")

    def test_mixed_valid_and_invalid_items(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            Graph([
                (ATTR, "valid", "value"),
                (99, "invalid", "item")
            ])
```

This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). When you do this, you should always include a **meaningful error message** to indicate what the source of the error is. This makes your code more readable and helps significantly with debugging. For situations where you know that the error source will be a certain type, you can choose to raise one of the [built in error types](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#base-classes), but should still include a meaningful message.\n\nThis particular exercise requires that you use the [raise statement](https://docs.python.org/3/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-raise-statement) to \"throw\" a `TypeError` for when a `Graph` is malformed, and a `ValueError` when an `Edge`, `Node`, or `attribute` is malformed. This takes a `list` of one \nor more tuples that describe:\n\n+ attributes\n+ `Nodes`\n+ `Edges`\n\nThe implementations of a `Node` and an `Edge` are provided in `dot_dsl.py`.\n\nFor more details on the DSL's expected design and the expected error types and messages, take a look at the test cases in `dot_dsl_test.py` \n\n\n## Exception messages\n\nSometimes it is necessary to [raise an exception](https://docs.python.org/3/tutorial/errors.html#raising-exceptions). When you do this, you should always include a **meaningful error message** to indicate what the source of the error is. This makes your code more readable and helps significantly with debugging. For situations where you know that the error source will be a certain type, you can choose to raise one of the [built in error types](https://docs.python.org/3/library/exceptions.html#base-classes), but should still include a meaningful message.\n\nThis particular exercise requires that you use the [raise statement](https://docs.python.org/3/reference/simple_stmts.html#the-raise-statement) to \"throw\" a `TypeError` for when a `Graph` is malformed, and a `ValueError` when an `Edge`, `Node`, or `attribute` is malformed. The tests will only pass if you both `raise` the `exception` and include a message with it.\n\nTo raise an error with a message, write the message as an argument to the `exception` type:\n\n```python\n# Graph is malformed\nraise TypeError(\"Graph data malformed\")\n\n# Graph item is incomplete (empty or incomplete tuple)\nraise TypeError(\"Graph item incomplete\")\n\n# Attribute has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Attribute is malformed\")\n\n# Node has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Node is malformed\")\n\n# Edge has incorrect values\nraise ValueError(\"Edge is malformed\")\n\n# Unknown item type\nraise ValueError(\"Unknown item\")\n```\n\n\nTask: Given a list of unittest methods, score each one's correctness vs the spec (0-100).\nReturn ONLY JSON mapping id->score, e.g., {\"m1\": 80, \"m2\": 55}."

[ARTIFACT_CONTENT_BEGIN] llm_response_31f72478-575e-4db9-9fb1-2f1cce9b1e0f_0_Qwen_Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct-FP8.txt
```python
import unittest
from main import *

class GeneratedTests(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})


    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"}), Node("b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])


    def test_malformed_graph_data_not_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")


    def test_malformed_graph_data_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph(None)
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_node_tuple_missing_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "a")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_edge_tuple_missing_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_attr_tuple_missing_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "foo")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_attribute_malformed_not_string_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_attribute_malformed_not_string_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 456)])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_node_malformed_not_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_node_malformed_not_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "a", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_edge_malformed_not_string_src(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_edge_malformed_not_string_dst(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", 456, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_edge_malformed_not_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([("UNKNOWN", "a", "b")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")


    def test_unknown_item_type_in_middle_of_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([
                (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
                ("UNKNOWN", "a", "b"),
                (NODE, "a", {})
            ])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")
```

```python
import unittest
from main import *

class GeneratedTests(unittest.TestCase):

    def test_empty_graph(self):
        graph = Graph()
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_node_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_edge_with_attrs(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {})


    def test_graph_with_one_attribute(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1")
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})


    def test_graph_with_multiple_items(self):
        graph = Graph([
            (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
            (NODE, "a", {"color": "green"}),
            (NODE, "b", {}),
            (EDGE, "a", "b", {"color": "blue"})
        ])
        self.assertEqual(graph.attrs, {"foo": "1"})
        self.assertEqual(graph.nodes, [Node("a", {"color": "green"}), Node("b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(graph.edges, [Edge("a", "b", {"color": "blue"})])


    def test_malformed_graph_data_not_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph("not a list")
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")


    def test_malformed_graph_data_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph(None)
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph data malformed")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_empty_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([()])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_node_tuple_missing_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "a")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_edge_tuple_missing_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_attr_tuple_missing_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "foo")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_attribute_malformed_not_string_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, 123, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_attribute_malformed_not_string_value(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", 456)])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_node_malformed_not_string_name(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, 123, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_node_malformed_not_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "a", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_edge_malformed_not_string_src(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, 123, "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_edge_malformed_not_string_dst(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", 456, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_edge_malformed_not_dict_attrs(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", "not a dict")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_unknown_item_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([("UNKNOWN", "a", "b")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")


    def test_unknown_item_type_in_middle_of_list(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([
                (ATTR, "foo", "1"),
                ("UNKNOWN", "a", "b"),
                (NODE, "a", {})
            ])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_node_tuple_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, "a", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_edge_tuple_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", "b", {}, "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_attr_tuple_extra_element(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "foo", "1", "extra")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_node_malformed_name_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(NODE, None, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Node is malformed")


    def test_edge_malformed_src_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, None, "b", {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_edge_malformed_dst_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(EDGE, "a", None, {})])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Edge is malformed")


    def test_attribute_malformed_key_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, None, "value")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_attribute_malformed_value_none(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([(ATTR, "key", None)])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Attribute is malformed")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_node_not_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([NODE])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_edge_not_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([EDGE])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_graph_item_incomplete_attr_not_tuple(self):
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError) as context:
            Graph([ATTR])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Graph item incomplete")


    def test_unknown_item_empty_string_type(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as context:
            Graph([("", "a", "b")])
        self.assertEqual(str(context.exception), "Unknown item")
```

Looking at the requirements and test cases, I need to implement the `Graph` class to handle a DSL similar to Graphviz's DOT language. The class should parse tuples representing nodes, edges, and attributes, with proper validation and error handling.

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        # Handle empty or None data
        if data is None:
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
        
        # Check if data is a list
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
        
        # Process each item in the data
        for item in data:
            # Check if item is a tuple and not empty
            if not isinstance(item, tuple) or len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
            
            # Process based on item type
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
            
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(dst, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
            
            elif item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                if not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
            
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}

        if data is None:
            return

        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")

        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple) or len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            item_type = item[0]

            if item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))

            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(dst, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))

            elif item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                if not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value

            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

I'll implement the Graph class to pass all the tests. Here's the complete implementation:

```python
# main.py
NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: dict):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: dict):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: list | None = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            data = []
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

This implementation:

1. Initializes empty nodes, edges, and attrs collections
2. Handles None input by treating it as an empty list
3. Validates that the input data is a list
4. Processes each item in the data list:
   - Validates each item is a tuple
   - Validates each item has the correct length for its type
   - Validates the types of all components (strings for names/keys/values, dicts for attributes)
   - Raises appropriate exceptions with correct messages for all error cases
5. Builds the graph by adding nodes, edges, and attributes to their respective collections
6. Handles all the test cases including:
   - Empty graph
   - Graphs with nodes, edges, and attributes
   - Malformed graph data
   - Incomplete items
   - Invalid types for components

The implementation strictly follows the specification and should pass all the provided test cases.

Looking at the failing test and the error message, I can see that the issue is in the `Graph` class constructor. The test `test_empty_graph` is calling `Graph()` with no arguments, but the current implementation is raising a "Graph data malformed" error.

Let me analyze the problem:

1. The test expects `Graph()` to create an empty graph with empty nodes, edges, and attrs
2. The current code raises an error when no data is provided
3. The constructor needs to handle the case where no arguments are passed

Looking at the test cases, I can see that:
- `Graph()` should create an empty graph
- `Graph(data)` should process the data list
- Various error conditions need to be checked

Here's the corrected implementation:

```python
# main.py
NODE = "node"
EDGE = "edge"
ATTR = "attr"


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name, attrs=None):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs or {}

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src, dst, attrs=None):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs or {}

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.src == other.src and self.dst == other.dst and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data=None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
            
        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple) or len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py

NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name, attrs):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src, dst, attrs):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data=None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}

        if data is None:
            return

        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")

        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            item_type = item[0]

            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value

            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))

            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))

            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Any

NODE = "NODE"
EDGE = "EDGE"
ATTR = "ATTR"


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name: str, attrs: Dict[str, str]):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"Node({repr(self.name)}, {repr(self.attrs)})"


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src: str, dst: str, attrs: Dict[str, str]):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)

    def __repr__(self):
        return f"Edge({repr(self.src)}, {repr(self.dst)}, {repr(self.attrs)})"


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data: List[Tuple] = None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        if data is None:
            return
            
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")

        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
                
            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
                
            elif item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
                
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

Looking at the failing test `test_malformed_graph_data_none`, I can see that the Graph constructor should raise a TypeError when None is passed as the graph data, but it's not currently doing so.

Let me analyze the requirements and fix the implementation:

```python
# main.py
NODE = "node"
EDGE = "edge"
ATTR = "attr"


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name, attrs=None):
        if not isinstance(name, str):
            raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
        if attrs is None:
            attrs = {}
        if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
            raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return isinstance(other, Node) and self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src, dst, attrs=None):
        if not isinstance(src, str):
            raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
        if not isinstance(dst, str):
            raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
        if attrs is None:
            attrs = {}
        if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
            raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return isinstance(other, Edge) and self.src == other.src and self.dst == other.dst and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data=None):
        # Check if data is malformed (None or not a list)
        if data is None or not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
        
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        # Process each item in the data
        for item in data:
            # Check if item is a tuple and not empty
            if not isinstance(item, tuple) or len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
            
            # Get the item type
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == NODE:
                # Node requires exactly 3 elements: (NODE, name, attrs)
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                _, name, attrs = item
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
            
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                # Edge requires exactly 4 elements: (EDGE, src, dst, attrs)
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                _, src, dst, attrs = item
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
            
            elif item_type == ATTR:
                # Attribute requires exactly 3 elements: (ATTR, key, value)
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                _, key, value = item
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
            
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")

```

```python
# main.py

NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name, attrs):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src, dst, attrs):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data=None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}

        if data is None:
            return

        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")

        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            item_type = item[0]

            if item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))

            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(dst, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))

            elif item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                if not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value

            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
class Node:
    def __init__(self, name, attrs):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src, dst, attrs):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


ATTR = "ATTR"
NODE = "NODE"
EDGE = "EDGE"


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data=None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}

        if data is None:
            return

        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")

        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            item_type = item[0]
            if item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
            elif item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

Looking at the failing test `test_malformed_graph_data_none`, I can see that the Graph constructor should raise a TypeError when None is passed as the graph data, but it's not currently doing so.

Let me analyze the requirements and fix the implementation:

```python
# main.py
NODE = 1
EDGE = 2
ATTR = 3


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name, attrs=None):
        if not isinstance(name, str):
            raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
        if attrs is None:
            attrs = {}
        if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
            raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return isinstance(other, Node) and self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src, dst, attrs=None):
        if not isinstance(src, str):
            raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
        if not isinstance(dst, str):
            raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
        if attrs is None:
            attrs = {}
        if not isinstance(attrs, dict):
            raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return isinstance(other, Edge) and self.src == other.src and self.dst == other.dst and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data=None):
        # Check if data is None or not a list
        if data is None or not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")
        
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}
        
        # Process each item in the data
        for item in data:
            # Check if item is a tuple and not empty
            if not isinstance(item, tuple) or len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
            
            # Get the item type
            item_type = item[0]
            
            if item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))
            
            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))
            
            elif item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value
            
            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```

```python
# main.py

NODE, EDGE, ATTR = range(3)


class Node:
    def __init__(self, name, attrs):
        self.name = name
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return self.name == other.name and self.attrs == other.attrs


class Edge:
    def __init__(self, src, dst, attrs):
        self.src = src
        self.dst = dst
        self.attrs = attrs

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.src == other.src and
                self.dst == other.dst and
                self.attrs == other.attrs)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self, data=None):
        self.nodes = []
        self.edges = []
        self.attrs = {}

        if data is None:
            return

        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise TypeError("Graph data malformed")

        for item in data:
            if not isinstance(item, tuple):
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            if len(item) == 0:
                raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")

            item_type = item[0]

            if item_type == NODE:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                name, attrs = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Node is malformed")
                self.nodes.append(Node(name, attrs))

            elif item_type == EDGE:
                if len(item) != 4:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                src, dst, attrs = item[1], item[2], item[3]
                if not isinstance(src, str) or not isinstance(dst, str) or not isinstance(attrs, dict):
                    raise ValueError("Edge is malformed")
                self.edges.append(Edge(src, dst, attrs))

            elif item_type == ATTR:
                if len(item) != 3:
                    raise TypeError("Graph item incomplete")
                key, value = item[1], item[2]
                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(value, str):
                    raise ValueError("Attribute is malformed")
                self.attrs[key] = value

            else:
                raise ValueError("Unknown item")
```
